## Relationship method

Each repository receives one primary classification and at least five reciprocal connections. Links are generated reproducibly from public names, descriptions, portfolio order and a curated cross-domain adjacency map:

- **Shared semantic kernel** — meaningful non-generic terms overlap.
- **Domain peer** — the repositories address the same primary research problem.
- **Portfolio continuity** — adjacent work preserves a time-ordered research thread.
- **Cross-domain bridge** — an explicit bridge joins neighbouring constellations.

**Evidence boundary:** a portfolio link records semantic proximity or research continuity. It does not by itself establish software dependency, interoperability, empirical validation, external adoption, endorsement, production fitness or shared legal status. Repository descriptions and licence labels are time-bounded public metadata; each repository's own files remain authoritative.
